| **W-K (detail)** | **Verified wire-gaps — Sprint 224 deep-analysis (file:line confirmed by independent root-cause trace)** | ⚠️ **4 new-actionable + cross-refs** | **🆕 NEW-ACTIONABLE (each ≤200 LoC, distinct files, parallel-safe):** **(1) Nervous spawn-lock → blocks safe re-enable.** `executor.ts:241` `handleApprove` is a **timeout-less** Promise (only `pendingApprovals.set`, waits forever for `deckent nervous accept`) — unlike `handleSuggestTimeout:162` which has `setTimeout`. The fix exists: `panic-gate.ts` `awaitPanicGateApproval` (hard 10s timeout → auto-proceed, SAFETY_FLOOR excepted) but is **0-caller** (its own header: "wire is a follow-up"). → wire panic-gate timeout into the executor/spawn path. **F-home: F3-009 + 224-010.** **(2) Cost gate blind to `auth_mode`.** `start.ts:324` runs `estimateSprintCost` but never passes `billingMode`; `cost-calculator.ts:213` returns `costUsd=0` for `subscription`; claude `default_billing_mode:"subscription"` (`pricing-data-baseline.json:10`) → an API-mode dev burns real tokens while the `$5` cap estimates `$0` and never fires. → bridge `auth_mode→billingMode` + add mid-sprint token-usage abort (TokenSpikeDetector only fires in RETRO, post-hoc). **F-home: F6 + F1-010.** **(3) Windows-native backend.** `resolveBackend('auto')` always → `'docker'` with **no `process.platform` check** (spawn-backend.ts:253), and docker spawn uses `spawnSync('sleep')` → breaks on win32. → `win32→subprocess` branch + Node-timer instead of `sleep`. **F-home: cross-platform (§4 Trinity / §9 gates).** **(4) Provider failover unwired.** `provider-overflow.resolveWithOverflow` (subs→API tier-preserving overflow, opus→gpt-5) is **0-caller**; a fallback resolver DOES read `config.fallback_provider` (`provider.ts:609`) but is not wired into the live spawn/FIX path — FIX (`mid-sprint-adapter.ts`) reroutes agent/skill, never `task.provider` → same-provider retry on 429/timeout. → wire overflow + fallback-resolver into spawn-error/FIX. **F-home: F1-010.** **✅ CROSS-REF (already-acknowledged, NOT new debt):** worker-side `checkWorkerAuthority` (`worker.ts:602` unconditional `return true`, 0 prod caller) = **ADR-037 V1.0 advisory posture by design** — auditor-side `authority-enforcer.checkAuthority` IS active (don't conflate); ## 8. Business / Launch / OSS

- **Model:** MIT, free forever, self-hosted. No paid tier, no feature gate, no `deckent.app` account. Enterprise capabilities (multi-tenant, mTLS, k8s, SSO, SIEM, compliance) ship under the same MIT license — **not** a separate Enterprise Edition.
- **Beta:** v1.0.0-beta.1, OSS public beta window **2026-06-01**. First `npm publish` is **manual by Alperen** (policy: `feedback_npm_publish_user_approval`).
- **Distribution:** `npm install -g deckent`; VitePress docs site; public repo flip (`VerhexIO/deckent`) — monorepo vs split decision pending; sensitive-data scrub (git-filter-repo) before flip.
- **Marketing channels:** Show HN, Reddit (r/LocalLLaMA, r/programming, r/opensource), Twitter/X, Turkish dev community, Discord, Dev.to, landing page + demo video.
- **Comparison (capability, not disparagement — Alperen 2026-06-02):** the only OSS tool combining multi-agent parallel execution + sprint lifecycle + scope enforcement + memory/learning + multi-provider + MCP-native, in **one MIT product** that serves both the individual (native `deckent` REPL, zero-config) and the enterprise (multi-tenant, RBAC, audit, ERP — same codebase, **NOT a gated Enterprise Edition**, ADR-033). We compare on merits with Devin, Cursor, Claude Code, Cowork, Perplexity and open agent CLIs; we do not position as "anti" any of them. Tagline: **"Open source for open world."**
- **Single-product, single-codebase model (decided 2026-06-02; refined 2026-06-11):** NOT a separate product, NOT a fork, NOT two codebases. **TEK kod tabanı** — enterprise yetenekleri **aynı repo'da modüler, eklenebilir katman** (`core` + `enterprise-layer`). **Lisans rafinmanı (Alperen 2026-06-11):** community çekirdek **MIT/ücretsiz** (bireysel/temel kullanımın hiçbiri ödeme-duvarı arkasında değil); **enterprise modül FARKLI lisanslı** (MIT değil), aynı kod tabanında lisansla etkinleşir. Bu ADR-033 ihlali DEĞİL — "Enterprise edition yasak"ın gerekçesi "iki kod tabanı + topluluk bölünmesi"ydi; bu yapı tek-kod-tabanını korur. **ADR-033 amendment'i YAPILDI (Sprint 281)** — artık "pending" değil.
- **🆕 MOD-SPLIT — Community/Pro modüler split stratejisi (Alperen 2026-06-10, EN SON değerlendirilecek iş maddesi):** Enterprise taraf çok gelişti → modülerleşmeye gidilecek. **deckent community** (MIT, free) + **Enterprise sürüm AYRI** — modüler, community sürüme **lisansla doğrudan eklenebilir** (isimlendirmeler örnek: `deckent` ücretsiz repo + `deckent-pro` kurumsal; sonra kararlaştırılır). Ana fikir: **modülleri ayırmak, kurumsal tarafı ayrı lisanslamak** → ürün geliri + danışmanlık geliri, community sürümle bilinirlik. **Yapı (Alperen 2026-06-11, netleşti):** ayrı ürün/fork DEĞİL, **AYNI kod tabanında modüler `enterprise-layer`** (eklenebilir/lisansla-etkinleşen) — 2-repo fikri DÜŞTÜ, tek kod tabanı + modüler katman. Community=MIT, enterprise-modül=farklı lisans (ADR-033 amendment Sprint 281). **Sıralama: mevcut arc'lar (REPL/dashboard tam-işlevsellik, publish-readiness) bittikten SONRA, en son iş olarak değerlendirilir.** Gerektirdiği ön-işler o gün tasarlanır: modül sınırı envanteri (enterprise-layer dosya haritası — **temel: ADR-024/026 god-object→modüler split, ADR-008 tek-yönlü import**), lisans-yükleme mekanizması, 2026-06-02 single-product kararının ve ADR-033'ün resmî amendments'ı, positioning/web metin revizyonu. **ADR-temeli:** kademeli god-object split (ADR-024/026) bu modülerleştirmenin altyapısıdır (bkz. ADR-026 forward-link).
- **Growth target:** million users (god-level, no-MVP scope).
- **DeckentHub (skill marketplace, shipped seed):** `deckent-hub/skills/` holds the 20-skill seed set (spotify-control, telegram-bot, calendar-google, email-imap, weather-forecast, rss-reader, web-scraper, github-issues, slack-notifier, notion-sync, todoist, spotify-playlist, youtube-downloader, reddit-fetcher, twitter-post, screenshot-vision, file-organizer, currency-converter, translator, discord-moderator). `deckent skill publish` = sandbox + Ed25519 sign (`src/core/signature.ts`, @noble/ed25519) + registry push. Hub is a local directory, not yet flipped to a separate `VerhexIO/deckent-hub` repo.
- **DeckentHub growth + governance (planned):** signing infra done, CI `validate-skill.yml` scaffolded, `rating-system.ts` present. **Not built:** moderation queue, CI auto key-rotation, phased registry growth 20→50→100 with vector search. Post-beta maturation track.
- **OSS publish pipeline — decisions outstanding:** Done: `.gitignore` excludes `.brain/`/`.tasks/`/`.locks/`, `package.json bin.deckent`, validate-publish engine/entry-point gates. **Undecided/not built:** monorepo-vs-split flip, sensitive-info scrub (no git-filter-repo/BFG/gitleaks pre-commit), final npm package name, `.github/ISSUE_TEMPLATE/`, PR template, `FUNDING.yml`, landing page.
- **AEGIS public standard track (post-beta, deferred):** ADR-061 Phase 5 names `agentaegis.io` (open standard repo), an AEGIS-compliant-orchestrator certification program, and academic papers (ICSE/FSE 2027, NeurIPS 2026 multi-agent track). Not built — no domain, spec draft, or paper artifacts; deferred until AEGIS Phase 1–4 ship.

## 9. Beta Gates (status as of 2026-06-01)

20 of the original gates pass: `tsc` clean, vitest **18,390 passed / 58 skipped (1,021 files) + dashboard 570 passed (0 failures)**, coverage, all MCP tools (32) + CLI commands (49+) functional, `npm pack` clean, cross-platform (macOS/Linux/WSL2), multi-provider abstraction, i18n, Memory V2 stress, zero CRITICAL/HIGH debt, ADR governance, Brain stability, synthetic-NO_GO disk-verify gate.

Recently closed (Sprint 192–211):
- **Synthetic NO_GO KAYNAK 6+7 closure** — ✅ both timeout-synthesis (`gateSyntheticTimeoutResult`) and graceKill panic-guard (`gateSyntheticGraceKillResult`) now call `verifyDiskAgainstClaim`, emit `DISK_VS_CLAIM_MISMATCH`, and reclassify to MANUAL_REVIEW_REQUIRED on disk contradiction. Disk-verify gate 100% closed across both paths.
- **memory.db sprint-log finalize fix + backfill** — ✅ Sprint 197 missing-row bug fixed (`sprint-finalizer.ts` defensive `upsertSprintLog`); reconstruction tool `scripts/backfill-sprint-log-rows.mjs`.

Conditional/open:
- **✅ CI-CD GREEN (months-broken → FIXED Sprint 214, commit `b67c000`):** all GitHub Actions workflows pass — CI (Type Check, Security, every test shard, **Coverage Report 5m15s**, **Build 28s**), Docs, Cross-Platform E2E, Secret Scan. Coverage Report + Build now actually run (were never reached for months). **Root-cause family: green-local ≠ green-CI** (8 fixes): (A) non-hermetic tests reading gitignored `.deckent/config.json` / `.brain/memory.db` → skip-if-absent / MemoryStore mock (`spawn-backend-docker`, `nervous-faz1-smoke`, `tools.test` retro); (B) brittle assertion (`task-builder-skill` `===3000`→`>=3000`); (C) blocking `spawnSync` freezing the vitest worker (`dead-code-audit` → async spawn); (D) **Coverage-job teardown RPC starvation** on the 2-core runner (forks serialising v8 coverage at teardown) → `pool:'forks'` + `maxForks:2` under CI + `teardownTimeout:30s`; (E) 7 vitepress dead-links → GitHub URLs / absolute paths. Full pattern map: memory `project_ci_green_root_causes`. **Remaining hardening (Sprint 215):** `npm run test:ci-sim` (clean-state local reproducer) + CI-hermeticity lint + ci-guardian/ci-testing routing so this never silently regresses.
- **Multi-provider runtime** — abstraction ready; docker backend Claude-only, tmux/subprocess support Codex/Gemini (full docker parity = F1-004/005).
- **Messaging trio** (Discord/Telegram/WhatsApp) — scaffold present, token activation pending.
- **M1–M4 monitoring baseline auto-blocker** — ⬜ not built. Post-beta observability gate.
- **Documentation sync** — MASTER-PLAN consolidation done; remaining: README badge (190+ → 214+), unverified "96% context reduction" claim (no benchmark file), CLAUDE.md/DECKENT.md module-count re-sync (§3).

## 11. Anchor Rules / DNA / Governance (unchanged)

- **No MVP, ever** — god-level scope; ask "is this god-level?" (`feedback_no_minimum_no_mvp_deckent`).
- **Disk-verify ground truth** — trust the disk, not Brain verdict or worker self-assessment (`feedback_trust_brain_eval_not_worker`).
- **Subscription-first** — API mode forbidden during beta (`project_api_mode_deferred_post_beta`).
- **npm publish = manual Alperen** (`feedback_npm_publish_user_approval`).
- **Karpathy 4-discipline** worker anchor (Think / Simplicity / Surgical / Goal-driven).
- **Wire proof must measure the goal, not the letter** — exclude def-file from caller greps; wire-task scope must include the caller (`feedback_directive_kanit_letter_vs_goal`).
- **Memory budget** 900 lines max in `.brain/`.
- **MASTER-PLAN = her zaman güncel proje defteri** (Alperen 2026-06-04) — planlanan / yapılan / devam-eden **TÜM** iş burada kayıtlanır: brainstorm'lanan tasarımlar §-bölümü (örn. §4A AS-2), alt-sistemler §4B haritası, sprint'ler §10 satırı. Dağınık dokümana değil, **buraya**. Bu, "single source of truth" kuralının operasyonel hâli.
- **ADR-033** Product-Not-Service · **ADR-037** RBAC authority matrix · **ADR-041** agent taxonomy · **ADR-040** nervous system · **ADR-062** embedded terminal · **ADR-074** native chat + enterprise + evolution.

---

## 12. Top Risks

1. **F5 stays dormant** — entry-points without callers read as "DONE" but ship dead code; the evolutionary moat is unproven until F5-004 lands real callers. *Mitigation: Sprint 212 priority + per-sprint visible-change evidence.*
2. **Routing collapse** — agent diversity is fragile/non-deterministic; specialization value lost if everything routes to `refactorer`. *Mitigation: skill→agent activation signal (212-008; frontend-design→frontend-designer mapping still incomplete — Sprint 213 plan sent UI to architecture-planner).*
5. **✅ Auth-precedence bug (RESOLVED Sprint 214):** `spawn-backend-docker.ts` env-forwarding is now provider+auth-aware — `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is stripped for Claude subscription workers and only forwarded when `authMode=api`. Sprint 215+ launches without `env -u`. ADR-076 Part A. ([[feedback_container_auth_precedence]])
3. **Native-chat scope creep** — IDE extension + Path A + Path C is multi-sprint; risk of half-built surfaces. *Mitigation: strict sequence (hygiene → IDE → web → SDK), one surface fully landed before the next.*
4. **Doc-reality drift** — this consolidation fixes today; re-drift if future status lands only in scattered docs. *Mitigation: MASTER-PLAN is the only roadmap that gets status updates; others are frozen historical.*
6. **✅ Non-hermetic tests / CI red (RESOLVED Sprint 214, commit `b67c000`):** green-local ≠ green-CI (tests reading gitignored `.deckent/config.json` / `.brain/memory.db`, blocking spawnSync, 2-core coverage-teardown RPC starvation) is FIXED — full CI green incl. Coverage + Build. *Residual risk: regression if new tests re-couple to local state; mitigated by Sprint 215 `test:ci-sim` + CI-hermeticity lint.* (memory `project_ci_green_root_causes`)

---

## 13. Explicitly Out-of-Scope (considered & deferred — recorded for zero-loss)

Items surfaced during the Sprint 211 doc-consolidation audit that were intentionally **not** added to the active plan, with reasons (so nothing silently vanishes):

- **Cloud-hosted SaaS offering** — rejected by ADR-033 (Product-Not-Service); permanent non-goal.
- **Microsoft-ecosystem core integration** (Graph / Teams / Outlook / Excel / Word / SharePoint) — from the Copilot analysis; considered & **deferred as optional, post-GA, non-core**. May ship as opt-in `connectors/` (the existing Discord/Telegram/WhatsApp pattern) but must NOT become a core direction — it would dilute the provider-free / self-hosted / anti-Devin DNA. Not an "Enterprise Edition."
- **LangSmith / external trace SaaS** — rejected: violates "never calls home". Use self-hosted OTel + own trace-graph (W-J) instead.
- **"Enterprise Operating Layer" positioning** — the Copilot framing; Deckent stays "install-and-run, MIT, self-hosted", not a managed enterprise platform. Enterprise *capabilities* ship under MIT (no gated edition).
- **Extra provider adapters** (Groq, Fireworks, Together, litellm), **embeddings/RAG**, **SWE-bench harness**, **monorepo planner**, **skill template gallery**, **blog campaign** — P3+/aspirational; Claude/OpenAI/Google/Ollama footprint meets beta GA.
- **`deckentd` daemon, Electron tray, native-window framework** — redundant vs Tauri/PWA + embedded terminal; out of scope.
- **Vector DB, Devin-style wiki semantic indexing, multi-model critique layer, browser/computer-use, deploy capability, progressive-disclosure UX, intent-classifier learning loop, hardware-attested HMAC** — post-GA vision/competitive-gap items with no current code foundation.
- **Verified non-issues** (claimed bugs that don't exist): memory-rebuild CLI split (Bug Z3 — semantics already correct), `auditor.md` PATTERNS.md regression (template clean), dedicated `brain-self-update.ts` module (hooks already dispersed correctly).
- **Already-adequately-represented partials** (Reversibility/EffectClass, TaskType extensibility, ADR-055 Hybrid Scoring, ADR-060 Self-Awareness, Nervous Phase 2/3, context-aware routing, rule-evolver) — folded into existing §3/§4/§5/§7 status text rather than duplicated.

Original spec follows.
  > **(orig spec)** Today goNogo/verify is too generic/hardcoded toward TypeScript ("tests pass", "tsc clean") and blind to BOTH the task kind AND the project's real stack. Brain must **derive** the go/no-go criteria + rubric + the worker's verify instructions from the task's `TaskKind` (WM-2) × `EnvironmentType` (WM-3) × `RequirementProfile` (WM-4) AND the detected project stack (`deckent_analyze_project`): e.g. code(ts)→tsc+vitest; code(c++)→cmake/ctest; code(py)→mypy/pytest; **doc→disk-verify only (no tests)**; **mail→send/delivery confirmation**; data-pipeline→schema/row checks; audit→findings-written. This is the **evaluation-layer generalization of MF-1** (which fixed only the doc-task PROMPT) + the full realization of ADR-053 (TaskKind), ADR-070 (Evaluation Integrity / zero-hard-code), ADR-019 (Language-Agnostic Worker Verify). Touch points: `extractGoNogoCriteria` (criteria generation), `rubric-registry` (per-kind rubric — already TaskKind-aware via WM-2b), `result-evaluator` (the eval gate — `isDocTask` is the seed), `prompt-god-template` (the worker verify section — MF-1 doc-gate is the seed), stack detection. **Without this, deckent only works well for TS code projects** — the "everyone everywhere, any task type" vision (mail/ERP/C++/python/data) requires it.

Folds with F1-005 (image contents) + PSL-6 (provider login/auth-probe). Doctor surfaces image-readiness alongside provider-login state.

- [ ] **F1-DF** 🔴 **HIGH — `Dockerfile.worker` is neither shipped in the npm package nor generated by init/onboard (Windows-install bug report, deckent 1.0.0-beta.1, 2026-06-19).** With `spawn_backend=docker`, deckent **mandates** a `deckent-worker:latest` image and instructs the user to build it via `docker build -f Dockerfile.worker -t deckent-worker:latest .` — but `Dockerfile.worker` is absent from a fresh `npm i deckent` install, so the docker backend is unusable out-of-the-box. **This is the prerequisite that BLOCKS F1-IMG** (the consent-rebuild command points at a source file that was never shipped) and folds with F1-005 (image contents). **Disk-verified ground truth (2026-06-19):** `package.json` `files[]` = `["dist","bin","README.md","LICENSE"]` → `Dockerfile.worker` (exists in dev-repo root, 2244 B, the F1-005 ca-certs-fixed one) is **NOT** included; `npm pack` ships only `dist/`+`bin/`+`README.md`+`LICENSE`. Five code paths all reference the un-shipped file: `worker-image-check.ts:157`, `spawn-backend-docker.ts:520`, `doctor.ts:1219`, `doctor-checks.ts:195`, `init-steps.ts:222`. **Repro:** `deckent config set spawn_backend docker` → `deckent doctor` → `[WARN] Worker image missing — Build: docker build -f Dockerfile.worker …` → `docker build -f Dockerfile.worker …` → **`Dockerfile.worker` does not exist.** **Fix (one of, god-level = ship the canonical file AND let deckent build it):** (a) ship `Dockerfile.worker` in `package.json` `files[]` (package root or `assets/`) so it's present on install; (b) a `deckent image build` command (or `deckent init` step) that **generates** the canonical Dockerfile from a packaged template + builds it (matches `worker-image-check.ts` expectations: claude/codex/gemini CLIs + `ca-certificates`, `INSTALL_CODEX`/`INSTALL_GEMINI` build-args); (c) wire `deckent doctor --fix-image` (F1-IMG) to generate-then-build instead of only pointing at a missing file. At minimum, document the canonical Dockerfile contents so users don't guess. **Workaround applied in the reporting project:** `Dockerfile.worker` hand-written (`node:24-slim` + `ca-certificates` + `git` + `@anthropic-ai/claude-code@2.1.177`) and `deckent-worker:latest` built → `doctor` then reported `[PASS] Worker image ready`.

- [x] **F1-TOK** ✅ **DONE (Sprint 273-275 — kapanış raporu: docs/alperen-analysis/2026-06-11-f1tok-closure-report.md; prefix-küçültme task-başı $0.52→$0.22, warm-spawn negatif-sonuç kapatıldı).** 🔴 **Token-optimizasyon core özelliği — "0 kayıp, daha az limit-yakımı" (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-10; veri: docs/alperen-analysis/2026-06-10-weekly-limit-reverse-engineering.md).** Limit tersine-mühendisliği bulgusu: haftalık abonelik limiti **model-fiyat-ağırlıklı maliyet** sayıyor (in·$in + out·$out + cacheWrite·1.25$in), **cacheRead pratikte BEDAVA** (~%0 ağırlık). Yakım kompozisyonu: cacheWrite **%57-63** (!), output %36-41, input ~%1; token hacminin %96'sı cacheRead ama limit etkisi sıfır. En büyük israf: **fleet cacheWrite = toplam limit yakımının ~%40'ı** — her worker session'ı TÜM context'i sıfırdan cache'e yazıyor (fable worker: 56 session × ~125K cw/session; cross-worker cache paylaşımı YOK — paralel spawn yarışı + system-prompt'taki git-status/heartbeat divergence şüphesi). İş kalemleri (içerik KISALTMADAN — `feedback_prompt_completeness_over_brevity` korunur): (1) **cache-warm spawn**: wave spawn'dan önce model başına 1 ucuz çağrıyla shared prefix'i cache'e yaz + worker başlatmalarını 2-3 sn stagger'la → sonraki worker'lar read-hit (bedava); (2) **prompt-layout stability**: blok sırası en-paylaşılandan en-task-özele (agent→skills→ADR→scope→task zaten doğru; system-prompt divergence kaynaklarını — git-status snapshot, timestamp — araştır/sabitle); (3) **TTL keep-alive**: uzun test koşusu/idle sırasında <5dk'da bir mini cache-read ping'i (TTL her kullanımda yenilenir; ping ≈ $0.0005 vs fable full-rewrite $1.56 — ~3000× ROI; heartbeat daemon'a doğal ek); (4) **FIX/retry session-resume**: yeni CLI session yerine `--resume` → bootstrap cw tasarrufu + bağlam sürekliliği; (5) **haiku çağrı-şekli fix**: hit %48-69 (diğerleri %95-98), 23-35K cw/istek — micro-call'ları batch'le ya da stable prefix ver (haiku/istek maliyeti sonnet'in 2 KATI çıktı); (6) **limit-ledger metriği**: maliyet-eşdeğeri birim (cacheRead hariç) deckent'in core usage-metriği olsun — sprint retro/status/dashboard'a "limit-yakım" kolonu, F1-LIM park-eşiği bu birimden beslensin. Hedef: cw %70 azalt (≈ toplam −%40) + output disiplini −%15-20 → **aynı haftalık limitle ~2× iş, sıfır kalite/içerik kaybı**. F1-LIM (dürüst durma/park) + F1-010 (overflow) ile üçlü: ölç → optimize et → taşır. **Resmi-doküman kıyası + 5-fazlı iş planı (TOK-LEDGER → PREFIX-STAB → CACHE-WARM → TTL-MGMT → OUT-DISC → A/B kanıt):** analiz dokümanı §8-9. ⚠️ **İki ayrı limit sistemi karıştırılmaz (Alperen 2026-06-10):** API rate-limits (istek/dk debi, 429+retry-after) ≠ abonelik usage limiti (5-saat+haftalık kota; formül yayınlanmamış — bizim 2-nokta kalibrasyon). Caching-MEKANİĞİ bulguları resmen belgeli ve doğrudan geçerli: paralel-istek cache-yarışı ("N parallel requests all pay full price; send 1, await first token, fire N−1") + max_tokens:0 prewarm + 5dk TTL + min-cacheable-prefix (fable/sonnet 2048, opus/haiku 4096) + model-scoped cache. F1-LIM tasarım gereği: 429→backoff, kota→PARK ayrı yollar. Somut repo bulgusu: heartbeat.pid/sprint.lock/memory.db.backup-* gitignore'da değil → git-status snapshot divergence → CC prefix bölünmesi (Faz 1 ucuz fix). **🆕 Geçmiş-sprint transcript analizi + canlı prompt denetimi (analiz §10, 2026-06-10 akşam):** 6 sprint / 69 task gerçek yakım — katmanlama kanıtı (fable-fleet $2,3-2,5/task → katmanlı $0,58-0,88), 269 kesinti+FIX-kaskadı $7,70/task (en pahalı israf = retry → F1-LIM #1), boot-cw fleet cw'sinin %44-63'ü, **`.result` tokenUsage beyanları gerçeğin ~%30'u (3-5× düşük) → ledger .result'ın YERİNE geçer + "tokenUsage eksik=NO_GO" kalkar**. Prompt denetimi (271-004: 85/100, 271-010: 90/100) → **🆕 Faz 1,5 PROMPT-CONS** (≥97 hedefi, sıfır-işlev-kayıplı 7 fix): goCriteria şablonundaki "`npx vitest run` passes" full-suite çelişkisi, persona "full test suite" × harness targeted-verify çatışması (bug-fixer 5 yer), açık `ADR-NNN` referansı topN'e zorla (271-004'te ADR-012 kaçtı, ADR-037 %48-balast girdi), ADR render dedupe + dev-ADR operative-extract, blok sırası Skills(paylaşılan)-first, `*Kanıt:**` interpolasyon artifact'ı. A/B hedefi güncel: task-başı ≤$0,45 kalite sabit.

- [x] **F1-RE** ✅ **VERIFIED (Sprint 254 live: codex banner "reasoning effort: high", claude script --effort xhigh) — Model reasoning-effort axis (depth) — distinct from task effort (work-size) (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09).** Done: `src/core/reasoning-effort.ts` (provider map + `resolveReasoningEffort`, opt-in+validated), `Task.modelEffort` + `- ModelEffort:` parse threaded, `ProviderCommandSpec.reasoningEffortArgs` + `buildProviderCommand`, spawn opts.reasoningEffort wired (docker + codex-host), `resolveWorkerEffort` lossy-cast fixed. Flags CLI-verified (claude `--effort`, codex `-c model_reasoning_effort=`). +11 tests. **claude-host wire COMPLETE (Sprint 254 hand-code):** `--effort` now flows on the full claude host path — `claude.ts buildCommand` (tmux+subprocess branches), `claude.ts spawn()`→`spawnWorker`, `tmux.ts SpawnOptions`+`buildWorkerCommand` (adapter-delegation + no-adapter fallback), `spawn-backend.ts TmuxBackend.spawn`, `subprocess.ts CLAUDE_SUBPROCESS_CONFIG.buildArgs`+`buildCommandString` — all validated via `resolveReasoningEffort('claude', …)` so invalid (e.g. codex-only `minimal`) levels are dropped. +11 host tests (claude/tmux/subprocess). **Flag+values LIVE-CONFIRMED (Sprint 254, claude 2.1.169):** `claude --help` shows `--effort <level>`; an invalid-value probe printed `Valid values: low, medium, high, xhigh, max` — exact match to `REASONING_EFFORT_BY_PROVIDER.claude`, and claude *gracefully ignores* an unknown value (warns + uses default, never crashes) so the wire is double-safe. ⬜ Remaining: config-level default model-effort (per-model sensible default). TWO different "effort" concepts must stay separate: **(A) task effort** (`- Effort: low|normal|high`) = WORK SIZE — drives timeout-estimator, sprint-estimator, cost token-estimate, skill-budget, prompt context. **Keep as-is, do NOT touch.** **(B) model reasoning-effort** = REASONING DEPTH the model itself offers (codex `reasoning_effort`, claude/opus `low|medium|high|xhigh|max`, "ultrathink"-class). **AUDIT (2026-06-09): (B) is NOT sent anywhere today** — codex logs show `reasoning effort: none` (default), claude uses its default; the existing `resolveWorkerEffort` (`task-builder.ts:1099`, returns max/high/medium/low) is wired to nothing AND has a lossy cast bug (`forceEffort as 'max'|'high'|'medium'|'low'` — `'normal'` isn't in the union). **Feature:** (1) when fetching/registering models (catalog, F6-005/F1-AD), also pull each model's available reasoning-effort levels onto the registry; (2) add a per-task `- ModelEffort:`/reasoning-effort override (DISTINCT from `- Effort:`); (3) send the resolved level to the CLI (codex `-c model_reasoning_effort=<x>`, claude effort flag, gemini if supported); (4) sensible default per model. Fix the `resolveWorkerEffort` lossy cast as part of this. Folds with F1-AD (catalog-driven model attributes). **Do not conflate work-size with reasoning-depth anywhere in code.**
- [x] **F1-CB** ✅ **DONE (Sprint 254 hand-code) — Cost billing-mode now follows auth_mode for codex/gemini (Alperen 2026-06-09 — "abonelikle bağlıyız, veri doğru olmalı").** The Sprint 248 cost estimate labelled codex/gemini tasks `(api)` and computed non-zero USD even though they run via OAuth/subscription (no API key). Root: the cost model assigned codex/gemini a static `api` `default_billing_mode` (legacy from when they required keys). **Fix:** new `resolveBillingModeForAuth(provider, effectiveAuthMode)` in `cost-calculator.ts` (ollama→`local`, subscription→`subscription`=$0, api→`api`, hybrid/unknown→`undefined`=provider-default); wired into all 3 cost-input maps (CLI start dry-run + gate, MCP start gate) via `t.authMode ?? await readAuthMode(root)` so the estimate FOLLOWS the effective per-task/config auth. +5 tests (subscription-codex=$0, api-codex>0, ollama→local, hybrid→default). Same bug-family as W-K(detail-2) #2 (cost gate blind to auth_mode), inverse direction.

#### B-MF. Mixed-Fleet Sprint-249 forensic fixes (2026-06-09, disk-verified via 3 parallel agents — full report: `docs/alperen-analysis/2026-06-09-mixed-fleet-sprint249-forensics.md`). Verdict: orchestration WORKED (4 real providers, ~13/15 correct deliverables on disk) but eval/routing/verify layers misfired (4 false-NO_GO + 8 timeouts). All hand-coded (self-modifying path).
> **P0 (MF-1/2/3) DONE + LIVE-VERIFIED (Sprint 250, `db4296a3`):** 4-task verify (1 per provider) → all 4 DONE on their REAL provider; **codex doc-task DONE (was false-NO_GO in 249) = MF-1 proof**; gemini+ollama ran host (no docker→claude degrade) = MF-2 proof; 320 tests + 6 new (3 MF-1 prompt, 1 MF-2 honest-fail, 2 MF-3 docker-guard), tsc clean.
- [x] **MF-1** 🔴 P0 ✅ VERIFIED **Tier-0 doc tasks must NOT run the full test suite.** `worker-default.md:27-31` Verify Loop is unconditional; Brain exempts doc tasks (`result-evaluator.ts:158` isDocTask→DONE) but `task-builder.ts buildWorkerPrompt` does not → shell-capable codex runs `npm test` (full 17k suite), collapses under its sandbox (EROFS `~/.codex/config.toml`, EPERM, API-timeouts, 63 pre-existing fails) → false-NO_GO + timeout despite a correct doc + passing lint+smoke. Caused **4 codex false-NO_GO + most of 8 timeouts**. Fix: add isDocTask/Tier-0 gate to buildWorkerPrompt suppressing/scoping the Verify Loop.
- [x] **MF-2** 🔴 P0 ✅ VERIFIED **No silent degrade-to-claude when a non-claude adapter is missing.** `sprint-spawner.ts:438` `isAdapterProvider(p)?getProviderAdapterForTask(p):null` → null falls to `else if(backend)`→docker→claude. ollama bootstrap availability race (`provider.ts:799-808`); gemini 010/013 docker-degraded (`workerId:docker-`, `provider:claude`) while 011/012 ran real → inconsistent. Fix: when isAdapterProvider=true but adapter null/throws → HONEST-FAIL (NO_GO "provider unavailable"), never silent claude degrade; lazy re-check availability at spawn; trace gemini 010/013 spawn-throw.
- [x] **MF-3** 🔴 P0 ✅ VERIFIED **Claude-only flags leak to non-claude CLIs (= WM-5 deferred part, now confirmed live).** gemini log: `Unknown arguments: dangerously-skip-permissions` → `--dangerously-skip-permissions` reached gemini → reject → fallback. Fix: gate claude-only args by provider in spawn arg-builder; never pass `--dangerously-skip-permissions`/claudeArgs to codex/gemini/ollama.
- [ ] **MF-4** P1 **Docker degradation hardcodes claude even when the task-provider CLI is in the image.** Probe: built `deckent-worker:latest` HAS claude+codex+gemini (ollama absent); `Dockerfile.worker:18,26-29` defaults claude-only (codex/gemini opt-in build-args). docker spawn invokes claude regardless. Fix (F1-004/F1-005): provider-aware docker CLI invocation + multi-CLI image incl. ollama + per-provider auth isolation — OR commit to hard host-routing for all non-claude.
- [ ] **MF-5** P1 **Result-format consistency.** `brainEvaluation` IS in main `.result` post-EVALUATE (`sprint-phases.ts:1193-1203`); `rubricScores` are intentionally audit-only in `.deckent/evaluations/` (`:1218-1225`) — DOCUMENT this (not a bug). Gaps: `-fix.result` lacks brainEvaluation (enrich it); codex tokenUsage=0; selfAssessment↔brainEvaluation contradictions (003/004 self=DONE/brain=NO_GO "rubric 0", rubric-bridge mis-score).
- [ ] **MF-6** P2 **ollama false-degrade warning over-fires + image gap.** The "routed to Docker… INCORRECT" warning fired ×2 even though 014/015 ran on real ollama (token evidence). Fix: warn only on genuine host-adapter failure; add ollama to image OR guarantee host-routing.
- [ ] **MF-7** P2 **FIX phase bounded (1×/sprint, 30-min cap, 3-reroute — `sprint-phases.ts:1622-1665`) but ineffective:** re-runs same workers with same verification → codex re-hits full-suite wall → re-NO_GO + re-timeout. Fix: change verification strategy on retry (apply MF-1); cap external verify time; skip re-run of disk-correct deliverables.
- [x] **MF-8** 🔴 P1 ✅ VERIFIED (Sprint 254 live: codex-docker DONE, no stub-flip) **honest-gate measurement false-positive (linesAdded=0) → false-NO_GO.** Sprint-249: 008 flagged `SCOPE_VIOLATION_OR_EMPTY_WRITE: linesAdded=0` despite a 109-line file. **Sprint-253 concrete repro (docker codex):** codex ran fully in-container, created a correct 3-line file (git diff shows +3), self-assessed DONE — but the honest-gate flipped it to NO_GO with `[honest-gate] DISHONEST_DONE_STUB: linesAdded=0 ... worker likely crashed` (it did NOT crash). The `linesAdded` measurement feeding the honest-gate under-reports (0) for non-claude / docker workers even though git-diff sees the change. Fix: reconcile the line-count measurement (use the same git-diff the EXIT-trap computes) before the honest-gate's stub/crash heuristic fires. This is the LAST blemish on the otherwise-fully-working codex-in-docker path. (Also: a suite test runs `npm run build` → deletes dist mid-vitest — test-hermeticity; + 63 pre-existing vitest failures to triage.)
#### B-PSL. Provider Spec Layer + Upgrade Channel (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09 — unifying design pillar; supersedes/parents MF-3/MF-4 + F1-004/005/012/AD/PD)
> **Design (confirmed correct):** worker-spawn must be **provider+model-aware, never claude-hardcoded** — including INSIDE docker. Today the docker backend builds one claude-CLI command shape (`-p -`, `--allowedTools`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions`) for every provider → codex/gemini break (MF-3 is a band-aid guard). The real fix is a **generalized spawn layer** that builds the right command per provider+model from a declarative spec. The critical concern is **provider volatility** — CLI commands/flags change (lived today: `gpt-5→gpt-5.5`, gemini `plan→yolo`/`--skip-trust`, `--dangerously-skip-permissions` rejection), structure changes, or a provider disappears. deckent's **core layer owns** the provider-command definitions; **users pull critical updates autonomously via `deckent upgrade`** so a provider change is fixed once centrally and propagates — no per-user spawn debugging. "Product not service" + zero-hardcode applied to the provider layer.
- [~] **PSL-1** Declarative `ProviderCommandSpec` (data, not code). — ✅ **CODE-COMPLETE P1+P2 (Sprint 252, `ed0f3fce`, hand-coded, pending live verify-sprint):** `src/core/provider-command-spec.ts` (binary/baseArgs/modelFlag/approvalArgs/allowedToolsFlag/promptFeed/oauthHomeDir + `buildProviderCommand` + built-in claude/codex/gemini map). Docker backend now builds the in-container command FROM the spec (no claude-hardcode) + mounts the per-provider OAuth dir (`~/.claude`/`~/.codex`/`~/.gemini`) so codex/gemini run live in docker (P2). codex/gemini `buildCommand` drift fixed (apiId + yolo/skip-trust). **CLI flags validated vs installed `--help`** (codex `--full-auto` deprecated → `--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox` for the container; gemini `yolo`/`--skip-trust`/`-o json`). gemini `buildCommand` yolo gated on autoApprove (security review). MF-3 band-aid → "no-spec (ollama) honest-fail". 6 new tests, 272 regression green, tsc clean. ⬜ **Pending:** (a) **verify-sprint hook** to route codex/gemini THROUGH docker (`- Backend: docker` per-task override OR `force_docker_providers` config — MF-2 routes them to host by default); (b) gemini docker **large-prompt shell-escaping** (`-p "$(cat …)"` is fragile for prompts with `"`; host buildArgs is shell-free/robust — follow-up); (c) full unification (host `buildArgs`/`buildCommand` derive from the same spec).
- [ ] **PSL-2** Capability/Contract layer (command ≠ contract): encode HOW each provider fulfills the worker contract — claude writes `.result` via prompt; ollama needs the node agentic-harness; codex/gemini are agentic CLIs. Ties to AS-4 Capability Realization Layer.
- [ ] **PSL-3** Versioning + spawn-time validation: probe the CLI before trusting an updated spec (reuses F1-AD detection); two drift axes — model names (F1-AD auto-detect) + command structure (spec + upgrade). Provider gone → bootstrap unavailable → honest-fail (MF-2), never silent degrade.
- [ ] **PSL-4** 🔴 `deckent upgrade` channel = supply-chain surface: provider-command specs are executable templates; pull ONLY from a signed/integrity-checked trusted source (deckent registry/release), never arbitrary. Autonomous spec-update must be signed + validated.
- [ ] **PSL-5** Graceful fallback chain: provider missing / spec broken → text-injection fallback (today's behavior) + honest-fail; never a silently-wrong provider. (MF-3/MF-4 become the docker-side realization of PSL-1; F1-004/F1-005 the image/invocation; F1-012 the registry; F1-AD the model axis; F1-PD the de-hardcode goal.)
- [ ] **PSL-6** **Provider login / OAuth-binding onboarding + doctor auth-state probe (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09 — "sonranın işi, not al").** For correct first-install: deckent's provider section should expose per-provider login/OAuth-binding (e.g. `deckent provider login <codex|gemini|claude>` wrapping `codex login` / interactive `gemini` auth / `claude` login). **Critical:** today `doctor`/detection reports a provider available on CLI *presence* (`--version`), but **CLI present ≠ logged in** — if a provider is not authenticated, its docker (and host) workers fail auth. doctor must probe the ACTUAL auth/session state per provider (not just `--version`) and surface "CLI present but NOT logged in" so the user binds OAuth before relying on it. Closes the GAP-4 deferral (detectCodex/detectGemini currently assume `session` on CLI presence). Folds with F1-AD (model auto-detect) + the PSL onboarding flow.
  - ✅ Sprint 270: doctor auth-probe (probeProviderAuth + logged-in vs logged-out distinction)
- [ ] **PSL-7** **Backend taxonomy clean-up (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09 — "ileride gerekli olursa diye şimdiden doğru kullan").** Spawn backends are currently `docker | tmux | subprocess` (config `spawn_backend`). There are really TWO orthogonal axes that should not be conflated: **process location** (docker-container vs host-process) × **inference location** (cloud — claude/codex/gemini APIs — vs local — ollama on-device). A `host` backend (rename `subprocess`→`host`, or add `host`) and a clean `cloud/host/local` vocabulary may be wanted later — deferred ("bekleyebilir"), but DO NOT invent a conflicting `host` value before then. The Sprint-252 `- Backend:` per-task override deliberately uses ONLY the existing `docker|tmux|subprocess` enum (no `host`), with honest per-task resolution (`task.backend !== config.spawn_backend` → resolve that backend). When the taxonomy is formalized, migrate the override + `isAdapterProvider` (host-adapter routing) onto it.
- [ ] **MF-9** P2 **sprint process clean-exit gap.** After the logical conclusion (RETRO written, "tamamlandı" logged, all workers gone), the main `deckent start` process lingered ALIVE for an extended period (host-adapter codex FIX-workers hit the 30-min cap; the controller did not exit promptly once they timed out). Fix: ensure the controller force-reaps timed-out host-adapter workers and exits cleanly at conclusion (no lingering process / orphan host CLIs). **Outcome (final tally):** Brain RETRO 21 attempts → 4 DONE / 7 TECH_DEBT / 10 NO_GO vs **14/15 deliverable files correct on disk** — the eval-vs-disk chasm is the core lesson; MF-1..MF-3 are the highest-leverage closes.

- [ ] **SPAWN-1** 🟡 **LOW (+ real security dimension) — Node `DEP0190` (`shell:true` + args) leaks to user stdout/stderr on Windows; arg-escape injection surface (Windows-install bug report, deckent 1.0.0-beta.1, Node v24, 2026-06-19).** `deckent doctor` (and any command that probes/spawns a CLI) prints `(node:…) [DEP0190] DeprecationWarning: Passing args to a child process with shell option true can lead to security vulnerabilities, as the arguments are not escaped, only concatenated.` Two harms: (1) deprecation noise bleeds into user output; (2) `shell:true` + unescaped concatenated args is a genuine command-injection surface (exactly why Node deprecated it). **Disk-verified trigger:** `detectCliVersion` (`src/core/provider.ts:346-350`) runs `spawnSync(cmd, args, { …, shell: isWindows })` — on win32, `shell:true` WITH an args array (`['--version']`) = the precise DEP0190 condition; `doctor` hits it on every provider probe (`detectClaude`/`detectCodex`/`detectGemini`). Second live site: `src/providers/subprocess.ts:159` (`shell:true` to resolve `.cmd`/`.ps1` wrappers like `claude.cmd` on the worker-spawn path). **ADR-006 tension:** `shell:true` is itself the ADR-006 (spawnSync Security Pattern) violation the `authority-enforcer.ts:470-487` lint flags — these two Windows sites are the known carve-out; DEP0190 now makes closing them a forcing function. **Fix (NOT a blind `shell:false` flip — Windows needs `.cmd`/`.ps1` resolution, a naive flip ENOENTs the npm-CLI wrappers):** resolve the wrapper's full path (PATHEXT / `where`) and call with `shell:false` + args array, **OR** invoke via `cmd.exe /c <cmd> <args>` with `shell:false` + array (Node escapes the array → no DEP0190, no injection). `src/core/worker-image-check.ts` already uses `spawn(…, { shell:false })` correctly — but note `docker` is a real `.exe` (simpler) than the `.cmd` provider wrappers, so reuse the pattern with the Windows wrapper-resolution caveat. Closes the deprecation noise AND the ADR-006 carve-out in one move. **Repro:** Windows + Node v24 → `deckent doctor` → DEP0190 at the head/tail of output.

- [ ] **CFG-1** 🟠 **MEDIUM — a legacy `mode` value on disk blocks ALL `config set`; read/validate/migrate are three-way inconsistent (Windows-install bug report, deckent 1.0.0-beta.1, 2026-06-19).** Plan modes were renamed `performance`/`balanced`/`economic`/`api` (legacy: `max_plan`/`max5x_plan`/`pro_plan`). With a legacy `"mode": "pro_plan"` in `.deckent/config.json`, **any** `deckent config set <unrelated-key> <value>` (e.g. `spawn_backend docker`) fails the whole-config schema validation with `Invalid value 'pro_plan' for field 'mode'` — the error fingers `mode` even though the user was setting an unrelated key, and the only escape is to manually run `config set mode economic` first. **Disk-verified ground truth — write-path rejection CONFIRMED in current tree:** `config set` (`src/cli/commands/config.ts:111-145`) loads the on-disk config, applies the new key, then calls `validatePartialConfig` → `validateConfig`, which checks `VALID_MODES` (`src/core/config.ts:370` = `['performance','balanced','economic','api']`, **legacy excluded**) and throws at `config.ts:488-489`. The **read** path normalizes legacy (`loadConfig`→`resolveMode`, `config.ts:1327`/`2118` — `config get mode` returns `economic`), but the **set/validate** path never calls `resolveMode` before validating → asymmetry. Self-contradiction in one file: the error string (`config.ts:489`) advertises the legacy names as "valid (legacy)" while `VALID_MODES` (`config.ts:370`) rejects them. **`config migrate` part — as-reported on beta.1, partially diverges in current tree:** the report says `config migrate` printed "Added 0 field(s)" and left `mode` legacy; in the **current** tree `migrateConfig` (`src/core/config-migration.ts:221-223`, since Sprint 096) DOES rewrite a top-level legacy `mode`→canonical and writes + backs up (`:288`), but the CLI message (`config.ts:256`) reports only `result.addedFields.length`, so a pure legacy-rename still reads as "Added 0 field(s)" (`MigrationResult` carries no `renamedLegacy` signal) — misleading, and needs a beta.1 re-verify for whether the rewrite itself was absent there. **Fix (god-level = make the three paths agree):** (1) normalize `mode` via `resolveMode` inside `validatePartialConfig` / the `config set` action BEFORE `validateConfig` (one line, mirrors the read path) → unblocks every `config set` AND persists the canonical value on the next write; (2) surface the legacy rename in `MigrationResult` + the `config migrate` message so users see `mode` was rewritten (not a silent "0 fields"); (3) keep the error message and `VALID_MODES` in sync (don't claim a value is "valid (legacy)" while validation rejects it). **Workaround (reporting project):** run `deckent config set mode economic` once, after which all other `config set`s succeed.
- [ ] **DOCTOR-1** 🟠 **MEDIUM — `deckent doctor` Platform check is backend-blind (FAIL + "Subprocess mode only" on Windows even when `spawn_backend=docker` works); + Brain-budget line mislabel (Windows-install bug report, deckent 1.0.0-beta.1, 2026-06-19).** **Part A (CONFIRMED in live path):** with `spawn_backend=docker` on Windows (Docker Desktop, works), `doctor` prints `FAIL Platform — Windows UNSUPPORTED for tmux backend — use WSL2 … Subprocess mode only.` while the SAME report shows `OK tmux — not required (docker backend)` and `Status: READY` — self-contradictory + misleading (docker users told they're "subprocess only"). **Disk-verified:** the live command is `doctor.ts` (`registerDoctor`, `index.ts:100`; `doctor-checks.ts` is the secondary base module — both carry the identical bug). `runDoctorChecks(root, providerNames?, spawnBackend?)` (`doctor.ts:1247`) ALREADY receives `spawnBackend` and threads it into `checkTmux(…, spawnBackend)` + `checkDocker(spawnBackend)` — but `checkPlatform()` is called with **no argument** (`doctor.ts:1249`), and `checkPlatform` (`doctor.ts:72` / `doctor-checks.ts:56`) hardcodes win32 → `passed:false` + the tmux-specific "Subprocess mode only" message regardless of backend (`required:false`, so it does NOT block READY — but still prints a FAIL icon + wrong guidance). **Fix (surgical, backend already in scope):** thread `spawnBackend` into `checkPlatform(spawnBackend)` and branch — Windows + `docker`/`subprocess` backend → PASS/INFO with a backend-correct message; only `tmux` backend on Windows → WARN/FAIL; drop "Subprocess mode only" wording when the active backend is docker. Apply to both `doctor.ts` and `doctor-checks.ts` copies. **Part B (brain budget label — verify exact Windows symptom):** `checkBrainBudget` (`doctor.ts:263` / `doctor-checks.ts:277`) and the format line (`doctor.ts:882`) render `"{n}/900 lines"`, but `n` is `getMemoryEntryCount` = `MemoryStore.totalCount()` — a **DB entry count, not a line count** (semantic drift after the ADR-088 DB-first migration; the 900 budget was a `.brain/`-file *line* budget per DECKENT.md). On a fresh checkout with no `.brain/memory.db` it returns 0 → `"0/900 lines"` reads as "no memory". Fix: relabel to entries (or reconcile the budget unit to DB entries post-ADR-088) so the doctor brain-budget line is truthful; confirm the count is sourced from the real DB. **Repro:** `deckent config set spawn_backend docker` → `deckent doctor` → `FAIL Platform … Subprocess mode only` under "Your System" alongside `OK tmux — not required (docker backend)` + `Status: READY`.
- [ ] **DOC-PKG-1** 🟡 **LOW — the published package ships README but NOT `docs/`, so every relative doc link in the shipped README 404s for npm users (Windows-install bug #02, deckent 1.0.0-beta.1, 2026-06-19).** The report flags `README → docs/guide/docker-backend.md` as missing from the package; **disk-verified the broader root:** `docs/guide/docker-backend.md` DOES exist in-repo (13.6 KB, created 2026-06-14, after beta.1 — so beta.1 also lacked the file itself), README links it (`README.md:345`) alongside `getting-started.md`/`first-sprint.md`/`chat-mode.md`/`multi-provider.md`/`config-reference.md` (`README.md:344-345`), but `package.json` `files[]` = `["dist","bin","README.md","LICENSE"]` → **the entire `docs/` tree is excluded from `npm pack`**, so ALL those README links resolve to nothing once installed (not just the docker one). Same packaging-root as F1-DF (over-narrow `files[]`). **Fix (one of):** (a) point the shipped README's doc links at the hosted docs site (absolute URLs — the project already has a VitePress site under `docs/.vitepress/`) so npm-README links resolve; or (b) ship a curated user-facing subset (`docs/guide/` + `docs/reference/`) in `package.json` `files[]` so the relative links work offline. Either way, a packaged-README link must never 404. Add a `lint`/`docs:test` guard that fails if a shipped-README relative link points outside `files[]`.

- [ ] **GA-2** Public product-repo flip with sensitive-info scrub (git-filter-repo/BFG + gitleaks pre-commit) + monorepo-vs-split decision.

- [x] **INT-1** Close the memory-loss saga — ✅ **CONFIRMED (Sprint 238, 2026-06-08):** a post-fix intact sprint kept memory/exports whole (memory.db 243→**246 grew**, no wipe; exports intact: decisions 108 adr-ref, memory.md 766 lines). Sprint-232 5-layer fix validated live; §4F saga closed.

### S. Product Surfaces & Operational Intelligence (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09 — multi-provider proven; now the surfaces + intelligence that win non-coders and enterprises)
- [x] **COMM-1** 🔴 **Worker-to-worker communication mechanism.** ✅ **DONE (Sprint 278)** — real comms layer wired: shared context / structured messages / handoff so workers coordinate without blocking. Config `worker_comms: { enabled, shared_memory_ttl_ms, inject_handoffs, inject_shared }` (Task 278-001). Shared-memory write-bridge (Task 278-002), prompt injection (Tasks 278-003/004), structured notes (Tasks 278-001/005), CLI visibility (Task 278-008), e2e smoke (Task 278-009), API docs (Task 278-010). Multi-agent disposition (Task 278-007). Remaining: flow/autonomous/Brain comms expansion + dashboard visibility (next sprint).
- [ ] **DESK-1** 🔴 **App (DESKTOP + MOBILE) + advanced real-time interactive dashboard (win the non-coder).** A first-class application on **both desktop AND mobile** + a live, instantly-interactive dashboard so users who don't read code can run/observe/manage deckent: **god-level design, interaction, speed, dynamic + customizable, enterprise-grade yet easily managed and understandable.** Builds on F7 (control-plane) + the embedded web terminal (ADR-062); supersedes/absorbs the formerly star-gated mobile item (PB-2 — now part of the core app vision, both platforms together). **Terminal → god-level for millions of users** too (the CLI/REPL surface must match). Primary surface to reach beyond developers.
- [ ] **TEAM-1** **Multi-provider TEAM subscriptions (OpenAI Team / Anthropic Team / …) — native + fast, RBAC-aware.** Simultaneous multi-provider is PROVEN (Sprint 248-254); extend to team-tier subscription accounts: native, fast operation across all these structures, integrated with RBAC (F4/F10) so a team's seats/roles map onto deckent worker auth. Builds on AS-2 per-worker auth + F1-RE.
- [ ] **ROUTE-1** **Flawless model/effort assignment, tracking & evolution.** Each provider+model has a real "work-force" profile; deckent must assign perfectly — WHICH task → WHICH model → WHICH model reasoning-effort (F1-RE) — and **track + improve** those assignments as it joins a project, so deckent *evolves correctly* per project (outcome-tracker + F5 evolution + WM-7 type-aware criteria feed this). The routing decision becomes a learned, auditable model+effort assignment, not a static heuristic.
- [ ] **FB-1** **deckent self-operation feedback loop (opt-in, ships OFF — recommend ON).** As users run deckent, it tracks metrics about **deckent's OWN operation** (NOT their project content) — e.g. "model X was good on this kind of project, here's how it performed, what I routed and why." A `deckent feedback` command sends this telemetry to us so WE improve the routing/eval model. **Defaults to FALSE; we prompt to enable** — more data → deckent improves faster (never-calls-home stays the default; this is explicit opt-in). **Sequencing: an idea to keep in mind, built AFTER the desktop app + dashboard (DESK-1).** Must respect the privacy/data-sovereignty pillar — operation metrics only, never project content.

- [x] **PLAN-INT-1** ✅ **DONE (Sprint 276 — directive-interrogator + plan --interrogate + /interrogate slash; LLM-zenginleştirme opsiyonel-kalan).** **Directive-Interrogation phase — pre-PLAN interactive product questioning (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-10, gstack comparison).** Today Brain treats DIRECTIVES.md as given truth — no layer challenges the *framing* before planning (gstack's `/office-hours` 6 forcing questions + `/plan-ceo-review` proved this catches wrong-problem builds before any code; "you said daily-briefing app, you described a chief-of-staff AI"). Add an **opt-in** interrogation step BEFORE PLAN, on existing surfaces (CLI/REPL/native chat/dashboard): Brain asks forcing questions about the goal (pain vs feature-request, narrowest shippable wedge, hidden capabilities, premises to challenge, effort alternatives), then proposes a revised DIRECTIVES draft for user approval. Never blocks the direct DIRECTIVES path (power users skip); i18n TR/EN via getMessage; feeds `deckent_set_directives`/planner unchanged downstream. Cross-ref: `brain_planning` modes, §6 chat surfaces, DESK-1.
- [x] **XVER-1** ✅ **DONE (Sprint 276 — cross-verify core + dispatch advisory-wire + outcome-feed, default-off; gerçek çok-provider PASS kanıtı opt-in-kalan).** **Cross-provider adversarial verification — verify with a DIFFERENT provider than the one that built (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-10, gstack comparison; gstack `/codex` second-opinion pattern).** Multi-provider routing is proven (Sprint 248-254) but verification is same-model today — a provider effectively reviews its own kind's blind spots. Add an **opt-in, config-gated (default off)** eval-layer step: for high-stakes tasks (security/auth, P0 fixes, `risk-tagged`), Brain dispatches an independent "try to REFUTE this result" verification task to a different provider/model (e.g. claude work → codex/gemini verify), adversarial prompt + disk-verify grounded, verdict feeds result-evaluator as advisory signal + outcome-tracker/ROUTE-1 learned assignments. Subscription-aware cost ($0 on OAuth fleets per F1-CB); honest-fail when no second provider available (never silent skip). Cross-ref: ROUTE-1, B-MF (eval-vs-disk lesson), ADR-070 (evaluation integrity), feedback_trust_brain_eval_not_worker.
- [x] **BOT-1** ✅ **(2026-06-15, `d4c53bb2`) Humanized bot-agent — DONE (core+wire; ollama-local zero-cost path, default-OFF, fail-safe).** See §4G "İLERLEME — Telegram Bot" notu. _Original spec ↓._ **Humanized bot-agent for connector notifications (🆕 Alperen 2026-06-09).** The Telegram bot pushes notifications today, but they are raw/structured — not human, not readable. Introduce a dedicated **bot-agent** (haiku or sonnet — cheap/fast) that the connector bots (Telegram, then Discord/WhatsApp) ALWAYS run through, so every notification + reply is rephrased into natural, human, conversational language. **User-customizable** (persona/tone/verbosity per user). Fixed-structure flows (sprint approval, checkpoint, alerts) must NOT bypass it — the bot-agent humanizes ALL of them. Critical for the non-coder/enterprise UX. Wires through `notification-dispatcher` + `connectors/*` + the existing agentic bot (`deckent bot listen`). Cross-ref §4G (human-interaction) + DESK-1.

Memory: project_ifs_erp_first_connector. **🟢 read-side LANDED (2026-06-15, read-first):** (1) **IFS Cloud OData driver** `src/core/erp/ifs/driver.ts` (projection-REST, IFS-IAM bearer, read-only, hermetik, 8 test) = seam'in ilk gerçek tüketicisi; (2) **modüler `src/core/erp/` modülü** (connector/handler/factory/index + per-vendor `ifs|odoo|sap|dynamics` klasörleri) — MOD-SPLIT'e hazır tek-yüzey; (3) **binding** `buildErpConnectorFromConfig` (config→driver→connector, driver-agnostic, opt-in, secret-from-env `tokenEnv`/`DECKENT_ERP_TOKEN` — credential asla config.json'da) **process-runtime + autonomous runtime-loop'a wire** (`{erp:{connector}}` → erp.read handler kurulur); (4) **real-binary kanıt**: `deckent serve` + config.erp(in-memory) → POST /api/process/submit erp.read → `status:"completed"`; her çağrı ENT-3 audit hash-chain'e düşer (training-data trail). Doküman: `docs/reference/enterprise-depth.md §12` (config şablonu + read-only sözleşme). Commit `103f99d4` (driver+reorg) + `e0ac534c` (binding). **Kalan:** (a) **gerçek IFS round-trip** = IFS test-ortamı creds + entity/projection haritası (post-beta, ARC-I); (b) **erp.write = AYRI ARC** — read-only invariant'ı 4 katmanda ters çevirir + geri-dönülmez harici-mutasyon (kayıt-sistemi) → CompiledMutation + per-vendor write-driver (CSRF/ETag/idempotency) + sert approval-gate (EffectClass critical-irreversible) + compensation/dry-run + ayrı write-credential; gerçek ERP test-ortamı olmadan doğrulanamaz.

## 16. NATIVE-AGENT PROGRAM — Strateji Pivotu (2026-06-12, Alperen yön)

> **Bu EKLENEN iş değil, YUTAN iş.** deckent terminali claude-CLI-wrap'ten ([[project_repl_architectural_root_cause]]) deckent'in KENDİ native-agent'ına geçiyor (mevcut REPL/Ink engine KABUL EDİLMEDİ). Bu yön, §15'teki ARC-C'nin chat/REPL/tool kümesini + F2/F11 kalanını + REPL-TOOL-DEBT ailesini **tek tutarlı yöne katlar** — net madde-yükü azalır, dağılır değil. İki katman ayrı: **orkestratör (Brain+worker) subs+API hibrit DEĞİŞMEZ**; yalnız **terminal** native olur.

**Bağlayıcı kararlar (bu oturum, memory'de tam):**
- Native-agent = deckent kendi agent-loop'u + provider'ın GERÇEK native tool_use'u (Anthropic `tool_use` / OpenAI fn-call / Ollama / vLLM-tool-parser), tag-parse hack DEĞİL. Transport: **API veya Ollama** (subscription raw-API vermiyor; o orkestratörde kalır). [[project_deckent_native_terminal_agent]]
- Provider-adapter **OpenAI-uyumlu-öncelikli** → BYO-key / Ollama / hosted-Deckent-Core hepsi tek arayüze takılır.
- Fable-determinizmi: API-pinned model-ID (auto-downgrade YOK); güvenlik-önlemi atlatma YOK.
- **Cross-check kuralı (bir süre bağlayıcı):** Anthropic↔OpenAI karşılıklı denetim, task-modunda. [[feedback_cross_check_anthropic_openai]]
- **Mimari yaklaşım: A** — temiz greenfield agent-core (`src/agent/`) + mevcut Ink view korunur.

**Sub-proje sırası (her biri kendi brainstorm→ADR→plan; biri diğerini bloke etmez):**
1. 🔜 **Native-terminal-agent core** [İLK SPEC — brainstorm yarıda: izin-modeli + kimlik + test bölümleri kaldı]. Yutar: ARC-C chat/REPL kalanı, F2-008, F11-014/016, REPL-TOOL-DEBT ailesi, izin-modeli (settings.local always-allow sızıntısı).
2. **Deckent Core fine-tune** (qwen3-14B/32B veya Hermes-4-14B base → QLoRA, deckent tool-trace + kod-tecrübe + ERP-enterprise). Yutar: [[project_ollama_worker_stub_gap]], deckent-qwen base-agent. [[project_deckent_core_model_and_provider]]
3. **Hosted Deckent Core + SDK** (vLLM OpenAI-uyumlu + API-key gateway → deckent kendi provider'ı; OpenRouter-mantığı Hermes-playbook). 3-tier: BYO / yerel / hosted.
4. **Opt-in telemetri-feedback** (🔴 GİZLİLİK-KRİTİK: default-off + açık-rıza + anonimleştirme + şeffaflık + air-gapped'de kapalı; sessiz-toplama ASLA).
5. **MCP-W1** (writer-lease split, çoklu-pencere -32000 fix) — bağımsız ama bu programla uyumlu.
6. **Temiz repo geçişi + docs-from-scratch** (ADR-065 yürütmesi): kodu `/docs/` hariç + doc-bağımlı testsiz temiz `deckent` repo'suna taşı → doc'ları koddan sıfırdan yaz; deckent-dev public→private. 🔴 Geçiş ÖNCESİ SP-2 eğitim-verisi (`.claude/projects`, `.brain/archive`, `.deckent`, `.tasks/archive`) ARŞİVLENMELİ (geri-dönülmez). [[project_clean_repo_migration_and_training_data]]

**Durum (2026-06-12, mola):** Brainstorm sürüyor (skill aktif); kapsam+transport+mimari-yaklaşım+model-stratejisi KİLİTLİ; kalan brainstorm-bölümleri = izin-modeli, deckent-agent kimliği/kuralları, test/migrasyon → sonra spec-yazımı → writing-plans.

**Durum GÜNCELLEME (2026-06-15, kod-doğrulamalı reconciliation — CC):** Yukarıdaki "brainstorm sürüyor" satırı ARTIK ESKİMİŞ. SP-1 native-agent core **M2→M4 zaten geldi** (flag-gated, default-OFF): `src/agent/{loop,session,identity,transport,permission*,provider-tooluse/{anthropic,openai,ollama},tools,guards}.ts` + `src/cli/repl/` Ink ağacı + `native-agent-bridge` + `--native`/`DECKENT_NATIVE_AGENT` flag + PTY harness. SP-2 eğitim-verisi pipeline (recorder+extractor, 54MB korpus) de DONE. SP-1 ~%72 bitmiş. SP-1-finish için kod-haritalı plan çıkarıldı (8 kalem, default-flip en sonda gated).
- ✅ **SP1-A1 DONE** (commit `88d9f0fc`, branch `sp1-native-agent-finish`): çekirdek-loop guard boşlukları kapatıldı — cost guard LoopDeps'e bağlandı (hard ceiling → tur mid-stream abort, advisory-only değil); `decide()`'a gerçek deny-rules geçiyor (eskiden `denies:[]` hardcoded); `RuleStore.activeDenies()` (permissions.deny); `loadPolicy` tierMap value-validation (geçersiz tier süzülür); ceiling config/env'den gerçek REPL path'ine plumb edildi (`DECKENT_NATIVE_COST_CEILING`/`native_cost_ceiling_usd`). TDD 6 yeni test, 1147 yeşil, tsc temiz.
- **Kalan SP-1 kuyruğu (sıra, FLIP-3 en sonda gated):** F11-016a Ink stabilizasyon (stream-segmenter unclosed-fence + queue/flush race) · F11-016b ADR-090 (ink+react vs ADR-010, ADR-010-W kapanışı) · FLIP-1 graceful subscription-fallback hint (fallback zaten ReplApp'te var; flip sonrası korkutucu-hata yerine soft-hint) · F11-014 codex first-class native (zaten `openai-compatible` ile fonksiyonel; native /provider-switch ince slice) · **F11-014 Gemini API adapter = DÜRÜSTÇE ERTELENDİ** (GOOGLE_API_KEY yok → kanıtlanamaz; key gelince) · FLIP-2 pre-flip verify-batch · FLIP-3 default-ON flip + claude-CLI spawn kes + tag-parse hack arşivle.
- **Karar (2026-06-15, Alperen):** Tam-5-provider parity hedef ama **provable-first**; native real-binary kanıtı şimdilik **ollama (lokal) + `DECKENT_NATIVE_MOCK`** ile (anthropic-api/openai/gemini key-gated). Mod = **CC el-kodu** (self-modifying terminal, ADR-039). FLIP-3 Gemini + parity-kanıt gelene kadar bekler.

## 17. DECKENT SDK — Gömülebilir Embed-Engine SDK (2026-06-16, Alperen yön)

> **Yeni arc — taslak-spec YAZILDI, implementasyon ERTELENDİ.** deckent'in KENDİ idiom'lu, gömülebilir TypeScript SDK'sı: geliştirici deckent'i kendi uygulamasına **orkestrasyon-motoru** olarak gömer. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-16-deckent-sdk-design.md`. Bu §16'dan AYRI bir yüzeydir: §16 deckent'in KENDİ terminalini native yapar (içe-dönük); §17 deckent'i BAŞKALARININ uygulamasına açar (dışa-dönük developer yüzeyi). [[project_deckent_sdk_spec]]

**Sıralama notu (Alperen, 06-16):** Modüler community/enterprise bölme işinden ([[project_community_pro_split_strategy]] / MOD-SPLIT) **ÖNCEYE alınabilir** — SDK kararlı bir public-kontrat + provider-adapter seam'i tanımlar; bu seam, enterprise-modül bölmesinin de doğal sınırı olur (önce kontratı çiz, sonra modülerleştir).

**Kilitli kararlar (brainstorm 06-16, D1–D5; spec'te tam):**
- **D1 Hero = embed-engine** (geliştirici deckent'i app'ine gömer; SaaS-client sonra ağ-üzeri ikiz).
- **D2 Katmanlı:** in-process core (güç-kullanıcı) + managed runtime (varsayılan, non-blocking, out-of-process); tek client yüzeyi, mod/transport değişir.
- **D3 En geniş kapsam:** orkestrasyon + gözlem + kontrol + memory(oku/yaz) + config/directives + **extensibility** (define provider/agent/skill/tool). MVP yok.
- **D4 Yaklaşım ①:** adanmış `src/sdk/` + transport-swappable client (`LocalTransport` ↔ `HttpTransport` = SaaS ikizi neredeyse bedava); mevcut motoru reuse eder (yıkmaz).
- **D5 Claude Agent SDK = config-gated provider seam** (ertelenmiş; `providers.claude_sdk.enabled`; kapalıyken peer-dep yüklenmez → ADR-010 korunur). Abonelik-politikası riskine karşı hedge. [[project_anthropic_subscription_credit_postponed]]

**§8 açık-sorular NETLEŞTİRİLDİ (06-16):** (1) Runner = `deckent serve` **reuse** (twin-örtüşme max). (2) define* cross-process = serileştirilebilir-metadata + module-specifier (handler IPC'den serileşmez → runner import eder); in-process doğrudan kayıt. (3) LocalTransport'ta da **bearer-token** (ephemeral, twin-parite). (4) Versiyon = v1 subpath `deckent/sdk` + `SDK_API_VERSION` sabiti + stabilite politikası; gerekirse ileride ayrı `@deckent/sdk`.

**Yeni ADR önerisi (impl başlarken):** "Deckent SDK Contract" — kararlı public yüzey + transport soyutlaması + extensibility modeli governed karar.

**Lossless-map:** §16 native-agent-program ile uyumlu ama ondan bağımsız (içe-dönük vs dışa-dönük); §15 arc'larından birine değil, **kendi §17 arc'ı** olarak kaydedilir.

---

> **Count:** §15'te 123 açık madde (13 arc). §16 native-agent pivotu bunların chat/REPL/tool alt-kümesini SUBSUME eder (eklemez) + 4 sub-proje (kendi spec-döngüleri). None is a throwaway/MVP cut — god-level. Build *sequence*: §15 öncelik-haritası + §16 program-sırası paralel.
